**Summary of GCP Outage**

* Service: AppEngine Flexible
* Region/Availability Zone: Multiple regions and availability zones were affected, including asia-east1, asia-east2, asia-northeast1, asia-northeast2, asia-south1, asia-southeast1, asia-southeast2, australiaintheast3, europe-central2, europe-west1, europe-west2, europe-west3, europe-west6, northamerica-northeast1, southamerica-east1, us-central1, us-east1, us-east4, us-west1, us-west2, us-west3
* Impact Date: 2024-10-09 14:57 UTC
* Duration: The outage lasted for approximately 9 hours and 21 minutes.
* Root Cause: The root cause of the outage was not specified in the provided text.
* Downtime Period: The downtime period started at 2024-10-09 17:18 UTC (resolved) and ended at 2024-10-09 17:52 UTC (mitigation applied).
* Resolved Date: 2024-10-09 17:18 UTC
